THIRD-PARTY LICENSE AND COPYRIGHT NOTICES

Collected from exact versions in source/package-lock.json and Cargo.lock. Cached archives were verified against their lock integrity/checksum. If a packed crate omitted a license file, the listed fallback is from its exact .cargo_vcs_info commit; selectors uses the official Mozilla MPL 2.0 text because its pinned source commit has no license text file. Duplicate payloads are included once and mapped to every package/version below.
